add_namespace = r56.ger
add_namespace = new_ger

country_event = { #Endsieg
	id = r56.ger.1
	title = r56.ger.1.t
	desc = r56.ger.1.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_german_reichstag_bombed

	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = GER
		has_civil_war = no
		date > 1940.1.1 #You don't get a bailout for being dumb early 
		ROOT = {
			#has_completed_focus = GER_total_war
			has_war = yes
			surrender_progress > 0.24
			casualties > 699999 #cheese prevention 
			NOT = {
				has_government = democratic
			}
			OR = {
				any_enemy_country = {
					is_major = yes 
					OR = {
						original_tag = ENG
						original_tag = USA
						original_tag = SOV
						original_tag = FRA
						original_tag = POL
						original_tag = ITA
					}
				}
				has_global_flag = had_yalta_conference
				AND = { #just incase the above stuff doesn't cause a fire and Germany is losing hard
					surrender_progress > 0.35
					casualties > 1200000 
				}
			}
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 4 }

	option = { #Final Victory
		name = r56.ger.1.a
		add_ideas = r56_endsieg_idea
		ROOT = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = r56_GER_endsieg_tt
		}
		set_country_flag = r56_GER_endsieg_event_happened
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
				}
				add_manpower = 20000
				add_fuel = 225
				add_offsite_building = { type = arms_factory level = 3 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 5000
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { 
					type = support_equipment
					amount = 235
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = light_tank_chassis
					amount = 25
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = medium_tank_chassis
					amount = 200
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = anti_air_equipment
					amount = 50
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = anti_tank_equipment 
					amount = 20
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = artillery_equipment
					amount = 100
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = motorized_equipment
					amount = 100
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = train_equipment
					amount = 15
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_manpower = 1250
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 1000
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = train_equipment
					amount = 5
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_tech = r56_militia_tech
					}
				}
				set_technology = {
					r56_militia_tech = 1
				}
				set_country_flag = r56_militia_formation_flag
			}
			load_oob = r56_GER_endsieg
			if = { #Cannon Endsieg units
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_full_control_of_state = 60
						casualties > 2000000
					}
				}
				load_oob = r56_GER_endsieg_units_1
			}	
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_full_control_of_state = 60
					}
				}
				load_oob = r56_GER_endsieg_units_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_full_control_of_state = 61
					}
				}
				load_oob = r56_GER_endsieg_units_3
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_full_control_of_state = 52
					}
				}
				load_oob = r56_GER_endsieg_units_4
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_full_control_of_state = 54
					}
				}
				load_oob = r56_GER_endsieg_units_5
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_full_control_of_state = 59
					}
				}
				load_oob = r56_GER_endsieg_units_6
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						has_full_control_of_state = 58
					}
				}
				load_oob = r56_GER_endsieg_units_7
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Pulled back from endsieg
	id = r56.ger.2
	title = r56.ger.2.t
	desc = r56.ger.2.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_german_troops

	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = GER
		ROOT = {
			has_war = yes
			has_capitulated = no
			surrender_progress < 0.02
			has_country_flag = r56_GER_endsieg_event_happened
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 3 }

	option = { #Closer to Final Victory
		name = r56.ger.2.a
		add_war_support = 0.03
		ROOT = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = r56_GER_endsieg_2_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = r56_GER_endsieg_event_pullback_happened
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = r56_endsieg_idea 
				}
				remove_ideas = r56_endsieg_idea 
			}
			delete_unit_template_and_units = {
				division_template = "Volkssturm"
				disband = yes 
			}
			delete_unit_template_and_units = {
				division_template = "Heeresgruppe"
				disband = yes 
			}
		}
	}	
}

country_event = { #Resolves Europe Post Yalta 
	id = r56.ger.3
	title = r56.ger.3.t
	desc = r56.ger.3.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_sign_treaty2

	fire_only_once = yes

	hidden = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Don't force anything on a player if possible!

		set_province_name = { id = 587 name = "Bonn"}
		51 = {
			set_victory_points = {
				province = 587

		if = { #If WW2 ends early this flag will push every tag to want to go for their nearest WW3 option in thier FT

		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = r56_yalta_script_is_a_go

	#	days = 42
	#}
	is_triggered_only = yes # Obsolete event, replaced by another

	option = { 
		name = r56.ger.4.a

			}

				}
				kill_ideology_leader = neutrality
				#add_ideas = GER_wilhelm_iv
			}

		}

			}
			every_country = {
				limit ={
					has_country_leader = { character = GER_wilhelm_iii  }
				}
				#swap_ideas = { remove_idea = GER_wilhelm_iii add_idea = GER_wilhelm_iv }
			}
			
		}		
		
	}
}

country_event = { #One last ai handout
	id = r56.ger.16
	title = r56.ger.16.t
	desc = r56.ger.16.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_german_reichstag_bombed

	fire_only_once = yes

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = GER
		is_ai = yes
		has_civil_war = no
		date > 1941.9.9 #You don't get a bailout for being dumb early 
		ROOT = {
			#has_completed_focus = GER_total_war
			has_war = yes
			surrender_progress > 0.88
			has_country_flag = r56_GER_endsieg_event_happened
			casualties > 799999 
			NOT = {
				has_government = democratic
			}
			OR = {
				any_enemy_country = {
					is_major = yes 
					OR = {
						original_tag = ENG
						original_tag = USA
						original_tag = SOV
						original_tag = FRA
						original_tag = POL
						original_tag = ITA
					}
				}
				has_global_flag = had_yalta_conference
				AND = { #just incase the above stuff doesn't cause a fire and Germany is losing hard
					surrender_progress > 0.90
					casualties > 1200000 
				}
			}
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 3 }

	option = { 
		name = r56.ger.16.a 
		if = {
			limit = {
				64 = {
					NOT = {
						is_controlled_by = GER
					}
				}
				has_global_flag = had_yalta_conference
				has_country_leader = {
					character = GER_adolf_hitler
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			kill_ideology_leader = fascism
			set_global_flag = r56_we_killed_no_no_man
			create_country_leader = { 
				name = "Karl Donitz"

				}
			}
		}

			relation = guarantee
			active = yes

			base = 50
		}

			fascism < 0.3
		}

		}

			}

			set_country_flag = GER_dem_anschluss_accepted
		}
		country_event = r56.ger.26 #world event
	}
	option = { #Yes, except the referendum doesnt pass because of fascists

		}

			base = 10
		}
		FROM = {
			country_event = r56.ger.21
		}
		country_event = r56.ger.21 #show them the referendum failed
	}
	option = { #No
		name = r56.ger.20.b

			base = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0

			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 8
				strength_ratio = {
					tag = GER
					ratio > 0.6 #very strong for its size
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				strength_ratio = {
					tag = GER
					ratio > 0.9 #we are the Germanic captain now
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ic_ratio = {
					tag = GER
					ratio < 0.4 #otherwise: maybe independence isnt so bad
				}
			}
			add_stability = -0.15
		}
		set_global_flag = dem_anschluss_refused #this has to be before the event for dynamic loc
		FROM = {
			country_event = r56.ger.21
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Germany civil war
	id = r56.ger.25
	title = erwache.5.t #reused from monarchists
	desc = r56.ger.25.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_german_reichstag_bombed

	fire_only_once = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	timeout_days = 7

	immediate = { # just in case we want to manually trigger this event without the focus
		hidden_effect = { 
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_variable = cw_dem_preview
					}
					NOT = {
						has_variable = cw_com_preview
					}
				}
				GER_civil_war_setup_and_preview = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Democrats
		name = r56.ger.25.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = GER_civil_war_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = r56_newline
		effect_tooltip = {
			var:cw_dem_preview = {
				change_tag_from = GER
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { num_equipment_in_armies_k@infantry_equipment < 20 }
			}
			set_global_flag = GER_invalidate_civil_war_achievement
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			var:cw_dem_preview = {
				GER_erwin_von_witzleben = {
					set_nationality = GER
				}
			}

			# only preview here, since the since we need to reapply this later on due to single tile territory assignment, which gets overwritten by the fascists
			GER_civil_war_make_this_democratic_preview = yes

			GER_civil_war_teleport_armies = yes

			var:cw_dem_preview = {
				GER_civil_war_make_this_fascist = yes
			}
			
			var:cw_com_preview = {
				GER_civil_war_make_this_communist = yes
			}

			GER_civil_war_make_this_democratic = yes
		
			clear_variable = cw_com_preview
			clear_variable = cw_dem_preview

			GER_civil_war_declare_wars = yes
		}
	}
	option = { #Commies
		name = r56.ger.25.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = GER_civil_war_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = r56_newline
		effect_tooltip = {
			var:cw_com_preview = {
				change_tag_from = GER
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { num_equipment_in_armies_k@infantry_equipment < 20 }
			}
			set_global_flag = GER_invalidate_civil_war_achievement
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = GER_communists_are_pretty_hard_tt
		#custom_effect_tooltip = GER_communists_are_pretty_hard_tt_tip

		hidden_effect = {
			var:cw_com_preview = {
				GER_ernst_thalmann = {
					set_nationality = GER
				}
			}

			# only preview here, since the since we need to reapply this later on due to single tile territory assignment, which gets overwritten by the fascists
			GER_civil_war_make_this_communist_preview = yes

			GER_civil_war_teleport_armies = yes

			var:cw_com_preview = {
				GER_civil_war_make_this_fascist = yes
			}

			var:cw_dem_preview = {
				GER_civil_war_make_this_democratic = yes
			}

			GER_civil_war_make_this_communist = yes

			clear_variable = cw_com_preview
			clear_variable = cw_dem_preview


			GER_civil_war_declare_wars = yes
		}
	}
}

			add_war_support = 0.1
			add_stability = 0.05
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
			GER = {
				add_to_war = {
					targeted_alliance = ROOT
					enemy = PREV
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #No
		name = r56.ger.29.b
		ai_chance = { 
			base = 0
			modifier = { #we can handle this
				add = 10
				NOT = {
					any_country = {
						has_war_with = ROOT
						is_major = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						}

					}
				}
			}

					}
				}
			}

			}

					}

					}
				}
			}

				elections_allowed = no

		}

		name = r56.ger.39.b

		}

						}
						neutrality > 0.3
					}
				}
			}
			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = neutrality
				size = 0.4
				GER = {
					puppet = PREV
					set_autonomy = {
						target = PREV
						autonomy_state = autonomy_dominion
						end_wars = no
						end_civil_wars = no
					}
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
					elections_allowed = no
				}
			}
		}
		else = {	#fascist coup
			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = fascism
				size = 0.4
				GER = {
					puppet = PREV
					set_autonomy = {
						target = PREV
						autonomy_state = autonomy_dominion
						end_wars = no
						end_civil_wars = no
					}
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = fascism
					elections_allowed = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

						is_in_faction = yes

				}

		}

			# 		has_war_with = ROOT
			# 	}

				1 = {

							}

							add_timed_idea = { idea = fascism_drift_ARG days = 1460 }

country_event = {
	id = new_ger.30
	title = new_ger.30.t
	desc = new_ger.30.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_europe_funeral
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		original_tag = GER
		has_completed_focus = GER_found_the_german_interim_government

		NOT = { has_completed_focus = GER_choose_an_heir_for_wels }
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = GER_kurt_spd
			has_country_flag = GER_vogel_spd
			has_country_flag = GER_carlo_spd
		}
	}
	#civil war starts
	option = {
		name = new_ger.30.a
		set_country_flag = GER_vogel_spd
		custom_effect_tooltip = GER_vogel_spd_tt
		IF = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = GER_chosen_gov_leader
					value = 5
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			promote_character = {
				character = GER_hans_vogel
				ideology = socialism
			}
		}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = democratic
			popularity = -0.05
		}
		add_to_variable = { SPD_seats = -5 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = GER_SPD_-5_tt
		unlock_national_focus = GER_choose_an_heir_for_wels
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1.75
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = new_ger.30.b
		set_country_flag = GER_kurt_spd
		custom_effect_tooltip = GER_kurt_spd_tt
		IF = {
			limit = {

					compare = equals
				}
			}

				ideology = socialism
			}
		}

			ideology = democratic

country_event = {
	id = new_ger.31
	title = new_ger.31.t
	desc = new_ger.31.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_soviet_soldiers_tank
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = new_ger.31.a
		set_variable = { GER_communist_support_before_puppet = party_popularity_100@communism }
		set_variable = { GER_democratic_support_before_puppet = party_popularity_100@democratic }
		set_variable = { GER_fascist_support_before_puppet = party_popularity_100@fascism }
		set_variable = { GER_neutrality_support_before_puppet = party_popularity_100@neutrality }
		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				original_tag = GER
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
			SOV = { 
				declare_war_on = {
					target = PREV
					type = topple_government
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = ROOT
				autonomous_state = autonomy_dominion
				end_wars  = no 
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
		}
		# Ensure Provisional government stays Non-aligned
		hidden_effect = {
			ROOT = {
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = communism
				}
			}
			# Reset popluarities to what they were before puppeting 
			set_popularities = {
				communism = GER.GER_communist_support_before_puppet
				democratic = GER.GER_democratic_support_before_puppet
				fascism = GER.GER_fascist_support_before_puppet
				neutrality = GER.GER_neutrality_support_before_puppet
			}
			clear_variable = GER.GER_communist_support_before_puppet
			clear_variable = GER.GER_democratic_support_before_puppet
			clear_variable = GER.GER_fascist_support_before_puppet
			clear_variable = GER.GER_neutrality_support_before_puppet
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = new_ger.55
	title = new_ger.55.t
	desc = new_ger.55.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_europe_funeral
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = { 
		set_country_flag = GER_willy_dead_flag
	}
	trigger = { 
		has_completed_focus = GER_the_old_man_still_has_it
		OR = {
			has_country_leader = { 
				character = GER_wilhelm_ii 
				ruling_only = yes 
			}
			has_idea = GER_wilhelm_ii_idea_new
			has_idea = GER_wilhelm_ii_2
			has_idea = GER_wilhelm_ii_3
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = new_ger.55.a
		remove_power_balance = {
			id = GER_wilhelm_ii_bop
		}
		IF = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { character = GER_wilhelm_ii ruling_only = yes} }
			kill_country_leader = yes
		}
		IF = { 
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = GER_remove_fatherlandless_fellows_from_power
				has_completed_focus = GER_reconcile_the_crown_prince
			}
			GER_wilhelm_iii = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					country_leader = {
						expire = "1965.1.1"
						ideology = absolute_monarchism
						traits = {
							GER_ties_to_the_nazis
						}
					}
					promote_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = GER_reconcile_the_crown_prince}}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_ideas = GER_wilhelm_ii_idea_new
				remove_ideas = GER_wilhelm_ii_2
				remove_ideas = GER_wilhelm_ii_3
			}
			complete_national_focus = GER_a_new_monarch_for_a_new_age
		}
		else = {
			hidden_effect = {

country_event = {
	id = new_ger.92
	title = new_ger.92.t
	desc = new_ger.92.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_german_politician_speech
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = new_ger.92.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				strength_ratio = {
					tag = GER
					ratio > 0.75
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				strength_ratio = {
					tag = GER
					ratio > 0.5
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				is_in_faction_with = GER
			}
			modifier = {	#sabotage then, so the chance is lower
				factor = 0.05
				has_global_flag = CZE_anschluss_sabotage_flag
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_idea = AUS_july_agreement
			}
			modifier = {	#shall always refuse Anschluss then!
				factor = 0
				AUS = {
					has_country_flag = protected_by_the_stresa_front_flag
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = AUS_west_allied
					has_country_flag = AUS_italy_allied
					is_in_faction_with = ITA
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			ROOT = {
				set_variable = { ROOT_democratic_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@democratic }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_democratic_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_communism_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@communism }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_communism_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_neutrality_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@neutrality }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_neutrality_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_fascism_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@fascism }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_fascism_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = democratic
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_democratic_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = neutrality
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_neutral_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = communism
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_communistic_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = fascism
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_fascist_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_wargoal_against = ROOT
					}
					remove_wargoal = {
					    type = all
				 	   target = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			set_autonomy = {
				target = ROOT
				autonomous_state = autonomy_integrated_puppet
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
			ROOT = {
				hidden_effect = {
					set_popularities = { #Denmark keeps it government form
						democratic = ROOT_democratic_popularity
						fascism = ROOT_fascism_popularity
						communism = ROOT_communism_popularity
						neutrality = ROOT_neutrality_popularity
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_democratic_flag
						}
						set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic }
						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_democratic_flag
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_neutral_flag
						}
						set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality }
						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_neutral_flag
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_communistic_flag
							}
						set_politics = { ruling_party = communism }
						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_communistic_flag
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_fascist_flag
						}
						set_politics = { ruling_party = fascism }
						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_fascist_flag
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_political_power = 150
		add_stability = 0.1
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { country_event = CHI_warlords.20}}
	}
	option = {
		name = new_ger.92.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 15
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}

				}

	}

country_event = {
	id = new_ger.150
	title = new_ger.150.t
	desc = new_ger.150.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_germany_politician_handshake
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = new_ger.150.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = GER_renegotiate_versailles_new }
		}
		add_political_power = 25
		add_war_support = 0.05
		set_country_flag = GER_sudetenland_demanded
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { #AI HANDICAP IS BACK!
				limit = {
					difficulty > 1
					GER = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
				}
				army_experience = 100
				add_offsite_building = {
					type = arms_factory level = 5
				}
				add_offsite_building = {
					type = industrial_complex level = 5
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 25000
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = artillery_equipment
					amount = 500
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment
					amount = 1750
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = motorized_equipment
					amount = 500
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = medium_tank_chassis
					amount = 500
					producer = GER
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = anti_air_equipment
					amount = 500
					producer = GER
				}			
			}
		}
		### EVENT CHAIN - MUNICH CONFERENCE (CZE is always involved) ###
		if = {
			limit = {
				CZE = {
					OR = {
						controls_state = 69
						controls_state = 1078
						controls_state = 74
					}
				}
			}
			if = { # HOL has formed EU and CZE is part of it - Alt-hist
				limit = {
					CZE = {
						is_in_faction_with = HOL
						is_subject = no
					}
					HOL = {
						has_completed_focus = HOL_foundations_for_a_european_union
						is_faction_leader = yes
						is_subject = no
						exists = yes
					}
					NOT = { has_war_with = HOL }
					NOT = { has_war_with = FRA }
					OR = {
						HUN = {
							NOT = {
								has_completed_focus = HUN_protect_czechoslovakia #DOD only
							}
						}
						CZE = {
							has_country_flag = HUN_AH_CZE_refused
						}
					}
				}
				HOL = {
					country_event = germany.40
				}
			}
			else_if = { #THIS IS THE HISTORICAL OUTCOME THAT SHOULD HAPPEN ON HISTORICAL - HISTORICAL HERE!! 
				limit = {
					ENG = {
						NOT = { has_completed_focus = ENG_a_change_in_course }
						exists = yes
						is_subject = no
					}
					NOT = { has_war_with = ENG }
					NOT = { has_war_with = FRA }
					CZE = {
						is_subject = no
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER }
						NOT = { has_war_with = ENG }
						OR = {
							controls_state = 69
							controls_state = 1078
							controls_state = 74
						}
					}
					OR = {
						HUN = {
							NOT = {
								has_completed_focus = HUN_protect_czechoslovakia #DOD only
							}
						}
						CZE = {
							has_country_flag = HUN_AH_CZE_refused
						}
					}
				}
				ENG = {
					country_event = germany.40
				}
			}

			### EVENT CHAIN - ALT-HIST DEMAND OF SUDETENLAND TO CZE OR THEIR OVERLORD ###
			else_if = { # If not historical and CZE is independent or GER subject, send event to them
				limit = {
					CZE = {
						OR = {
							is_subject = no
							is_subject_of = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				CZE = {
					country_event = { id = germany.122 }
				}
			}
			else_if = { # If not historical and CZE is subject of someone else, send event to overlord
				limit = {
					CZE = { is_subject = yes }
				}
				CZE = {
					overlord = { country_event = { id = germany.122 } }
				}
			}
		}

		### EVENT CHAIN - ALT-HIST SOME ONE ELSE CONTROLS SUDETENLAND STATES ###
		if = { #If other countries control Sudetenland states -> send event to them or overlord
			limit = {
				any_other_country = {
					NOT = { tag = CZE }
					OR = {
						controls_state = 69
						controls_state = 1078
						controls_state = 74
					}
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { tag = CZE }
					OR = {
						controls_state = 69
						controls_state = 1078
						controls_state = 74
					}
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
				}
				if = { # If independent or GER subject, send event to them
					limit = {
						OR = {
							is_subject = no
							is_subject_of = ROOT
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = germany.1041 days = 4 random_days = 2 }
				}
				else = { #If subject of someone else, send event to overlord
					if = {
						limit = {
							overlord = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
						}
						overlord = { country_event = { id = germany.1041 days = 4 random_days = 2 } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		add_named_threat = { threat = 5 name = GER_demand_sudetenland_threat }

		IF = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
				FROM = {
					is_ally_with = ROOT
				}
				FROM = {
					NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
				}
			}
			remove_from_faction = FROM
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_ally_with = FROM
				FROM = {
					NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
				}
			}
			leave_faction = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = new_ger.150.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		add_war_support = -0.05
	}
}

	}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = SAF_ethnic_legislation
				}
				uncomplete_national_focus = {
					focus = SAF_ethnic_legislation
					uncomplete_children = yes
				}
				unlock_national_focus = SAF_ethnic_legislation
				complete_national_focus = SAF_radicalize_the_afrikaner_broederbond
				complete_national_focus = SAF_celebrate_the_voortrekkers
				complete_national_focus = SAF_form_the_ossewabrandwag
				unlock_national_focus = SAF_solidify_german_contacts
			}
			remove_ideas = SAF_racial_segregation_2
			remove_ideas = SAF_return_to_republicanism
			add_ideas = SAF_racial_segregation
			add_ideas = ideological_fanaticism_focus
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = fascism
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = GER_saf_coup_tt
		set_cosmetic_tag = SAF_afrikaner_state
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
		IF = {
			limit = { is_subject = yes}
			overlord = {
				end_puppet = ROOT
				country_event = new_ger.176
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
		IF = {
			limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
			leave_faction = yes
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
		IF = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_faction_leader = yes}}
			FROM = { add_to_faction = ROOT}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { country_event = CHI_warlords.20 }
			
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
		}
		add_war_support = 0.05
		IF = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { controls_state = 119}
			}
			119 = { transfer_state_to = ROOT}
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = GER_ireland_deal_flag
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = GER_ireland_deal_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = GER
				enemy = ENG
				hostility_reason = asked_to_join
			}
		}
		FROM = { 
			add_to_faction = ROOT
			country_event = CHI_warlords.20 
		}
		add_ai_strategy = {
			type = alliance
			id = FROM
			value = 200
		}

	}
}
